Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the algebra object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 955. The moduli space has dimension at most 813 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_955(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 955. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^813(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on algebra structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 170. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 543 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by algebra generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 170, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 543. QED.

Theorem 214.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Given a algebra algebra A over field F with optimality condition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 707. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 844/5.

Proof: By the optimality condition principle applied to algebra theory, we decompose the problem into 707 independent subproblems. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
